[user message]
For each integer \(n\ge2\), let \(F_n\) be the free group on \(n\) generators. For any group \(G\), define its lower central series by \(\gamma_1(G)=G\) and \(\gamma_{r+1}(G)=[\gamma_r(G),G]\), where \([H,K]\) is the subgroup generated by the commutators \([h,k]=hkh^{-1}k^{-1}\) with \(h\in H\) and \(k\in K\). For every integer \(r\ge1\), define
\[
\mathcal A_r(n)=\ker\!\left(\operatorname{Aut}(F_n)\longrightarrow\operatorname{Aut}\bigl(F_n/\gamma_{r+1}(F_n)\bigr)\right),
\qquad \operatorname{IA}_n=\mathcal A_1(n).
\]
Determine, by explicit conditions on \(n\) and \(c\), the complete set
\[
\mathcal D=\left\{(n,c)\in\mathbb Z_{\ge2}\times\mathbb Z_{\ge1}:\ 
\gamma_{c+1}(\operatorname{IA}_n)\subsetneq
\gamma_c(\operatorname{IA}_n)\cap\mathcal A_{c+1}(n)
\right\}.
\]

The answer was proved recently, so it is not an open problem: your team's task is to find it and prove it, completely, and to write Python that really computes or checks every step that can be checked. Printing what you believe is not evidence. Arriving at an answer that settles less than the question asks (a partial or conditional answer, "not known") is not acceptable, and neither is conceding: when an approach fails, try another.

Proving it means showing that the answer is right in every case the question covers, and that no other answer is. Split the work, and the pieces of each part, between you on Slack.

Stop only when the team's proof is complete. Before you stop, post on Slack what you proved, and what is left.

```
from itertools import product

def add(a,b,scale=1,p=1000003):
 c=a.copy()
 for k,v in b.items():
  z=(c.get(k,0)+scale*v)%p
  if z:c[k]=z
  elif k in c:del c[k]
 return c

def apply(a,w,p):
 out={}
 for k,x in enumerate(w):
  for (i,v),t in a.items():
   if i==x:
    key=w[:k]+v+w[k+1:]
    out[key]=(out.get(key,0)+t)%p
 return {k:v for k,v in out.items() if v}

def bracket(a,b,n,p):
 out={}
 for (i,w),v in b.items():
  for t,z in apply(a,w,p).items():
   key=(i,t);out[key]=(out.get(key,0)+v*z)%p
 for (i,w),v in a.items():
  for t,z in apply(b,w,p).items():
   key=(i,t);out[key]=(out.get(key,0)-v*z)%p
 return {k:v for k,v in out.items() if v}

def generators(n,p):
 ans=[]
 for i in range(n):
  for j in range(n):
   if i!=j:ans.append({(i,(j,i)):1,(i,(i,j)):p-1})
  for j in range(n):
   for k in range(j+1,n):
    if i not in (j,k):ans.append({(i,(j,k)):1,(i,(k,j)):p-1})
 return ans

def basis(vectors,p):
 piv={}
 for a in vectors:
  a=a.copy()
  while a:
   k=min(a)
   if k in piv:a=add(a,piv[k],-a[k],p)
   else:
    inv=pow(a[k],-1,p)
    piv[k]={t:v*inv%p for t,v in a.items()}
    break
 return list(piv.values())

def ranks(n,maxdegree,p=1000003):
 gs=generators(n,p);bs=gs;out=[len(bs)]
 for d in range(2,maxdegree+1):
  bs=basis((bracket(a,b,n,p) for a in gs for b in bs),p)
  out.append(len(bs))
  print(n,p,d,len(bs),flush=True)
 return out
```

```
from pathlib import Path
text=r'''Diagnostic McCool calculation — NOT an IA_n result
====================================================
Let PΣ_3 be the subgroup generated by the six conjugations K_ij,
where K_ij conjugates x_i by x_j and fixes the other generators.
The standard McCool presentation has relators
 [K_ij,K_kj]=1 and [K_ij,K_ik K_jk]=1 for distinct i,j,k.
(The four-distinct-indices relators do not occur for n=3.)

Inn(F3) is normal in PΣ_3. Inner conjugation by x_j equals the
product of the two K_ij with i!=j. These two generators commute.
Modulo Inn(F3), set
 K31=K21^{-1}, K32=K12^{-1}, K23=K13^{-1}.
All the McCool relators become trivial on making these substitutions.
Consequently PΣ_3/Inn(F3) is free on the three remaining generators.
The quotient therefore splits, so PΣ_3 = F3 ⋊ F3.
The action on the inner F3 is induced by IA automorphisms and acts
trivially on its abelianization. Hence this is an almost-direct product.

For an almost-direct product K⋊H of free groups, commutator induction
from [H,K]⊂γ2(K) gives [γ_i(H),γ_j(K)]⊂γ_{i+j}(K).
Thus γ_c(K⋊H)=γ_c(K)⋊γ_c(H) for every c. As abelian groups its
lower central graded quotients are direct sums, so for PΣ_3 their
ranks are twice the Witt numbers for a rank-three free group:
 2/c * sum_{d|c} μ(d)3^(c/d).
In degrees 1..7 these are [6,6,16,36,96,232,624].

Code: workspace/agent4_mccool_check.py, which imports actual tensor
arithmetic from workspace/agent3_derivations.py.
For each prime it brackets degree-one derivations repeatedly and
performs modular Gaussian elimination on the resulting vectors.
CHECKED image ranks:
p=1000003: [6,6,16,36,96,231,618]
p=1000033: [6,6,16,36,96,231,618]
p=2:       [6,6,16,36,96,229,606]
p=3:       [6,6,16,36,96,231,615]

Important limitation: equality of two modular ranks is NOT an exact
proof of the corresponding rational rank. A nonzero modular minor
does prove a rational lower bound. An exact rational row reduction
or an exact dependency certificate is required for the upper bound.
This computation is diagnostic only; it is not used to infer ANY
kernel or rank statement for IA_3 or IA_n.
'''
Path('workspace/agent4_mccool_notes.txt').write_text(text)
print('wrote notes')
```

```
import sys,time
from fractions import Fraction
from pathlib import Path
sys.path.insert(0,'workspace')
from agent3_derivations import bracket
# Arithmetic via very large modulus isn't exact; use copied sparse tensor maps with no modulus.
def add(a,b,s):
 a=a.copy()
 for k,v in b.items():
  z=a.get(k,0)+s*v
  if z:a[k]=z
  elif k in a:del a[k]
 return a
def app(a,w):
 o={}
 for pos,x in enumerate(w):
  for (i,v),t in a.items():
   if i==x:
    k=w[:pos]+v+w[pos+1:];o[k]=o.get(k,0)+t
 return {k:v for k,v in o.items() if v}
def br(a,b):
 o={}
 for (i,w),v in b.items():
  for t,z in app(a,w).items():o[(i,t)]=o.get((i,t),0)+v*z
 for (i,w),v in a.items():
  for t,z in app(b,w).items():o[(i,t)]=o.get((i,t),0)-v*z
 return {k:v for k,v in o.items() if v}
def basis(vecs):
 piv={}
 for a in vecs:
  while a:
   k=min(a)
   if k in piv:a=add(a,piv[k],-a[k])
   else:
    c=a[k];piv[k]={t:Fraction(v,c) for t,v in a.items()};break
 return list(piv.values())
gs=[{(i,(j,i)):1,(i,(i,j)):-1} for i in range(3) for j in range(3) if i!=j]
bs=gs;out=[6]
for d in range(2,8):
 t=time.time();bs=basis(br(a,b) for a in gs for b in bs);out.append(len(bs));print(d,len(bs),time.time()-t,flush=True)
Path('workspace/agent4_exact_mccool_output.txt').write_text(repr(out))
# Save this self-contained exact computation.
Path('workspace/agent4_exact_mccool.py').write_text(Path('scratchpad.py').read_text().split('# Save this self-contained exact computation.')[0])
```

```
@lru_cache(None)
def words(d):return [w for w in itertools.product(range(3),repeat=d) if lyndon(w)]
# standard bracketing uses longest proper Lyndon suffix
@lru_cache(None)
def split(w):
 for i in range(1,len(w)):
  if lyndon(w[i:]):return w[:i],w[i:]
def K(i,j):return {(i,(j,i)):1,(i,(i,j)):-1}
inners=[add(K((j+1)%3,j),K((j+2)%3,j),1) for j in range(3)]
outers=[K(1,0),K(0,1),K(0,2)]
@lru_cache(None)
def evalw(w,inner):
 if len(w)==1:return (inners if inner else outers)[w[0]]
 u,v=split(w);return br(evalw(u,inner),evalw(v,inner))
ws=[w for w in words(6) if all(w.count(i)==2 for i in range(3))]

out=[{'inner':cols[i][0],'word':cols[i][1],'coefficient':str(v)} for i,v in relations[0].items()]
```

```
from functools import lru_cache
from math import factorial
from collections import defaultdict
import json,pickle,time
from pathlib import Path
D=8

def add(a,b,s=1):
 a=a.copy()
 for w,v in b.items():
  z=a.get(w,0)+s*v
  if z:a[w]=z
  elif w in a:del a[w]
 return a

def mul(a,b,degree=D):
 out=defaultdict(F)
 aa=defaultdict(list);bb=defaultdict(list)
 for w,v in a.items():aa[len(w)].append((w,v))
 for w,v in b.items():bb[len(w)].append((w,v))
 for i,terms in aa.items():
  for j,terms2 in bb.items():
   if i+j>degree:continue
   for w,v in terms:
    for x,t in terms2:out[w+x]+=v*t
 return {w:v for w,v in out.items() if v}

def inv(a,degree=D): # inverse of 1+a
 out={():F(1)};term={():F(1)}
 if not a:return out
 for k in range(degree//min(map(len,a))+1):
  term=mul(term,a,degree)
  if not term:break
  out=add(out,term,(-1)**(k+1))
 return out

def lyndon(w):return all(w<w[i:] for i in range(1,len(w)))
def split(w):
 for i in range(1,len(w)):
  if lyndon(w[i:]):return w[:i],w[i:]
@lru_cache(None)
def comm(w): # standard Lyndon group commutator in exp(letter), minus 1
 if len(w)==1:return {w*k:F(1,factorial(k)) for k in range(1,D+1)}
 u,v=split(w);a=comm(u);b=comm(v)
 delta=add(mul(a,b),mul(b,a),-1)
 return mul(mul(delta,inv(a,D-len(w))),inv(b,D-len(w)))
@lru_cache(None)
def lie(w):
 if len(w)==1:return {w:F(1)}
 u,v=split(w);return add(mul(lie(u),lie(v)),mul(lie(v),lie(u)),-1)

def decompose(poly):
 out={};a=poly.copy()
 while a:
  w=min(a)
  assert lyndon(w),(w,a[w])
  c=a[w];out[w]=c;a=add(a,lie(w),-c)
 return out

if __name__=='__main__':

 rel=json.loads((base/'agent4_mccool_degree6_relation.json').read_text())
 g={}
 for r in rel:
  assert not r['inner']
  g=add(g,comm(tuple(r['word'])),F(r['coefficient']))
 print('g components',{d:sum(len(w)==d for w in g) for d in range(6,9)},flush=True)
 p7={w:v*2 for w,v in g.items() if len(w)==7}
 c7=decompose(p7)
 assert all(v.denominator==1 for v in c7.values())
 print('correction terms',len(c7),'max',max(map(abs,c7.values())),flush=True)
 h={w:v*2 for w,v in g.items() if len(w)==8}
 for w,v in c7.items():h=add(h,{z:t for z,t in comm(w).items() if len(z)==8},-v)
 # h8 is logarithm of g^2 times correction group commutators.
 assert all(len(w)==8 for w in h)
 print('h8 terms',len(h),'denominators',sorted(set(v.denominator for v in h.values())),flush=True)
 with (base/'agent4_bch_candidate.pkl').open('wb') as f:pickle.dump((c7,h),f)
''')
exec(compile(p.read_text(),str(p),'exec'),{'__name__':'__main__','__file__':str(p.resolve())})
```

```
   if x==i:
    out[w[:pos]+(j,i)+w[pos+1:]]+=v
    out[w[:pos]+(i,j)+w[pos+1:]]-=v

@lru_cache(None)
```

```
from itertools import permutations
import sys

def polyadd(a,b,s=1):return add(a,b,s)
def mul(a,b):
 c={}
 for w,v in a.items():
  for z,t in b.items():c[w+z]=c.get(w+z,0)+v*t
 return {w:v for w,v in c.items() if v}
def bracket(a,b):return add(mul(a,b),mul(b,a),-1)
def K(i,j):return {(i,(j,i)):1,(i,(i,j)):-1}
def T(i,j,k):return {(i,(j,k)):1,(i,(k,j)):-1}
for i,j,k in permutations(range(3)):

 actual=add(br(add(K(k,j),K(j,k),1),T(i,j,k)),br(K(i,j),K(i,k)),-1)
 assert actual==expect,(i,j,k)
print('All six logarithmic degree-two identities verified exactly.')
```

```
############################################################################
##
#M IA group
##
############################################################################
InstallMethod( EmbeddingOfIASubgroup, "for a free group automorphism group",
        [ IsAutomorphismGroupOfFreeGroup ],
        function(A)
# conventions on generators:
# M[i][j][k] is M_{x_i,[x_j,x_k]} if all variables i,j,k are <= n. x_i^-1 is represented as i+n.
# M[i][j][k] is a variable only if j<k. If j>k, it is stored as M[i][k][j]^-1.
# C[i][j] is C_{x_i,x_j} if i,j <= n. If j>n, then it is C_{x_i,x_{j-n}}^-1.
#
# Beware that GAP composes maps left-to-right, while the article composes maps right-to-left.
# GAP's commutator is [g,h] = g^-1h^-1gh, while the article's commutator is ghg^-1h^-1.
# Therefore, in converting an article formula to a GAP formula, multiplication order must be reversed,
# and commutators must be switched from [g,h] to [h,g].
#
    local n, F, G, C, M, i, p, q, r, s, t, u, v, rels, endos, Gendos, epi, alpha;
    
    G := Source(One(A)); # the free group
    n := RankOfFreeGroup(G);
    
    C := List(Arrangements([1..n],2),p->Concatenation("C(",String(p[1]),",",String(p[2]),")"));
    M := [];
    for p in Arrangements([1..n],3) do
        if p[2]>p[3] then continue; fi;
        Add(M,Concatenation("M(",String(p[1]),",[",String(p[2]),",",String(p[3]),"])"));
    od;
    F := FreeGroup(Concatenation(C,M));
    i := 1;
    epi := [];
    C := List([1..n],i->[]);
    for p in Arrangements([1..n],2) do
        C[p[1]][p[2]] := F.(i);
        C[p[1]][p[2]+n] := F.(i)^-1;
        q := ShallowCopy(GeneratorsOfGroup(G));
        q[p[1]] := q[p[2]]*q[p[1]]/q[p[2]];
        Add(epi,GroupHomomorphismByImages(G,q));
        i := i+1;
    od;
    M := List([1..2*n],i->List([1..2*n],j->[]));
    for p in Arrangements([1..n],3) do
        if p[2]>p[3] then continue; fi;
        M[p[1]][p[2]][p[3]] := F.(i);
        M[p[1]][p[3]][p[2]] := F.(i)^-1;
        q := ShallowCopy(GeneratorsOfGroup(G));
        q[p[1]] := Comm(q[p[2]]^-1,q[p[3]]^-1)*q[p[1]];
        Add(epi,GroupHomomorphismByImages(G,q));
        i := i+1;
            
        M[p[1]][p[3]][p[2]+n] := M[p[1]][p[2]][p[3]]^C[p[1]][p[2]];
        M[p[1]][p[2]][p[3]+n] := M[p[1]][p[3]][p[2]]^C[p[1]][p[3]];
            
        M[p[1]][p[3]+n][p[2]+n] := M[p[1]][p[2]][p[3]+n]^C[p[1]][p[2]];
        M[p[1]][p[2]+n][p[3]+n] := M[p[1]][p[3]][p[2]+n]^C[p[1]][p[3]];
            
        M[p[1]][p[3]+n][p[2]] := M[p[1]][p[2]][p[3]]^C[p[1]][p[3]];
        M[p[1]][p[2]+n][p[3]] := M[p[1]][p[3]][p[2]]^C[p[1]][p[2]];
        
        for s in [0,n] do for t in [0,n] do
            M[p[1]+n][p[2]+s][p[3]+t] := Comm(C[p[1]][p[2]+s]^-1,C[p[1]][p[3]+t]^-1) / M[p[1]][p[2]+s][p[3]+t];            
            M[p[1]+n][p[3]+s][p[2]+t] := Comm(C[p[1]][p[3]+s]^-1,C[p[1]][p[2]+t]^-1) / M[p[1]][p[3]+s][p[2]+t];            
        od; od;
    od;
    
    rels := [];
    
    # R0: M(x_a^alpha,[x_b^beta,x_c^gamma]) * M(x_a^alpha,[x_c^gamma,x_b^beta])
    # not necessary anymore, since we put only half the generators
    if true then
    for p in Arrangements([1..n],3) do
        for s in Tuples([0,n],3) do
            Add(rels,M[p[1]+s[1]][p[2]+s[2]][p[3]+s[3]]*M[p[1]+s[1]][p[3]+s[3]][p[2]+s[2]]);
        od;
    od;
    fi;
    
    # R1: [C(x_a,x_b),C(x_c,x_d)]
    for p in Arrangements([1..n],2) do
        if p<>[1,2] then continue; fi;
        for q in Arrangements([1..n],2) do
            if p[1]<>q[1] and p[1]<>q[2] and p[2]<>q[1] then
                Add(rels,Comm(C[p[1]][p[2]],C[q[1]][q[2]]));
            fi;
        od;
    od;
    
    # R2: [M(x_a^alpha,[x_b^beta,x_c^gamma]),M(x_d^delta,[x_e^epsilon,x_f^zeta])]
    for p in Arrangements([1..n],3) do for s in Tuples([0,n],3) do
        if p{[1,2]}<>[1,2] then continue; fi;
        for q in Arrangements([1..n],3) do for t in Tuples([0,n],3) do
            if q[2]>q[3] then continue; fi;
            if p[1]+s[1]<>q[1]+t[1] and not p[1] in q{[2,3]} and not q[1] in p{[2,3]} then
                Add(rels,Comm(M[p[1]+s[1]][p[2]+s[2]][p[3]+s[3]],M[q[1]+t[1]][q[2]+t[2]][q[3]+t[3]]));
            fi;    
        od; od;
    od; od;
    
    # R3: [C(x_a,x_b),M(x_c^gamma,[x_d^delta,x_e^epsilon])]
    for p in Arrangements([1..n],2) do
        if p<>[1,2] then continue; fi;
        for q in Arrangements([1..n],3) do for t in Tuples([0,n],3) do
            if not p[1] in q and not q[1] in p then
                Add(rels,Comm(C[p[1]][p[2]],M[q[1]+t[1]][q[2]+t[2]][q[3]+t[3]]));
            fi;
        od; od;
    od;
    
    # R4: [C(x_c,x_b)*C(x_a,x_b),C(x_c,x_a)]
    for p in Arrangements([1..n],3) do
        if p{[1,2]}<>[1,2] then continue; fi;
        Add(rels,Comm(C[p[3]][p[2]]*C[p[1]][p[2]],C[p[3]][p[1]]));
    od;
    
    # R5: M(x_a^alpha,[x_b^beta,x_c^gamma])^C(x_a,x_b^beta) / M(x_a^alpha,[x_c^gamma,x_b^-beta])
    # not necessary anymore, since we put only a quarter of the generators
    if true then
    for p in Arrangements([1..n],3) do for s in Tuples([0,n],3) do
        if p{[1,2]}<>[1,2] then continue; fi;
        Add(rels,M[p[1]+s[1]][p[2]+s[2]][p[3]+s[3]]^C[p[1]][p[2]+s[2]]/M[p[1]+s[1]][p[3]+s[3]][p[2]+n-s[2]]);
    od; od;
    fi;

    # R6: M(x_a^-alpha,[x_b^beta,x_c^gamma])*M(x_a^alpha,[x_b^beta,x_c^gamma]) / [C(x_a,x_c^gamma)^-1,C(x_a,x_b^beta)^-1]
    for p in Arrangements([1..n],3) do for s in Tuples([0,n],3) do
        if p{[1,2]}<>[1,2] then continue; fi;
        Add(rels,M[p[1]+n-s[1]][p[2]+s[2]][p[3]+s[3]]*M[p[1]+s[1]][p[2]+s[2]][p[3]+s[3]]/Comm(C[p[1]][p[2]+s[2]]^-1,C[p[1]][p[3]+s[3]]^-1));
    od; od;
    
    # R7: [C(x_a,x_b^beta)^-1,M(x_a^-alpha,[x_b^beta,x_c^gamma])] / [C(x_a,x_d^delta)^-1,C(x_a,x_c^gamma)^-1]
    for p in Arrangements([1..n],4) do for s in Tuples([0,n],4) do
        if p{[1,2]}<>[1,2] then continue; fi;
        Add(rels,Comm(C[p[1]][p[2]+s[2]]^-1,M[p[2]+s[2]][p[3]+s[3]][p[4]+s[4]]^-1)/Comm(C[p[1]][p[3]+
... (1,127 characters cut) ...
1,2] then continue; fi;
        if p[4] in p{[1,3]} then continue; fi;
        Add(rels,M[p[3]+s[3]][p[2]+s[2]][p[1]+s[1]]*M[p[3]+s[3]][p[1]+s[1]][p[4]+s[4]]*
            M[p[3]+s[3]][p[1]+s[1]][p[2]+s[2]]^C[p[3]][p[4]+n-s[4]]/M[p[3]+s[3]][p[1]+s[1]][p[4]+s[4]]^C[p[1]][p[2]+s[2]]);
    od; od; od;
    
    endos := [];
    Gendos := [];
    # I_1
    t := []; u := [];
    for q in Arrangements([1..n],2) do
        Add(t,C[q[1]][q[2]]);
        if 1=q[2] then Add(u,C[q[1]][q[2]]^-1); else Add(u,C[q[1]][q[2]]); fi;
    od;
    for q in Arrangements([1..n],3) do for s in Tuples([0,n],3) do
        if q[2]>q[3] or s<>[0,0,0] then continue; fi;
        Add(t,M[q[1]+s[1]][q[2]+s[2]][q[3]+s[3]]);
        if 1 in q then s[Position(q,1)] := n-s[Position(q,1)]; fi;
        Add(u,M[q[1]+s[1]][q[2]+s[2]][q[3]+s[3]]);
    od; od;
    Add(endos,GroupHomomorphismByImages(F,t,u));
    t := ShallowCopy(GeneratorsOfGroup(G));
    t[1] := t[1]^-1;
    Add(Gendos,GroupHomomorphismByImages(G,t));
    
    # P_s for all permutations s on {1..n}
    for p in GeneratorsOfGroup(SymmetricGroup(n)) do
        t := []; u := [];
        for q in Arrangements([1..n],2) do
            Add(t,C[q[1]][q[2]]);
            Add(u,C[q[1]^p][q[2]^p]);
        od;
        for q in Arrangements([1..n],3) do for s in Tuples([0,n],3) do
            if q[2]>q[3] then continue; fi;
            Add(t,M[q[1]+s[1]][q[2]+s[2]][q[3]+s[3]]);
            Add(u,M[q[1]^p+s[1]][q[2]^p+s[2]][q[3]^p+s[3]]);
        od; od;
        Add(endos,GroupHomomorphismByImages(F,t,u));
        Add(Gendos,GroupHomomorphismByImages(G,Permuted(GeneratorsOfGroup(G),p^-1)));
    od;
    
    # M_{x_1^alpha,x_2^beta}
    for p in Tuples([0,n],2) do
        if p[1]=0 then alpha := 1; else alpha := -1; fi;
        t := []; u := [];
        for q in Arrangements([1..n],2) do
            Add(t,C[q[1]][q[2]]);
            if q[2]=1 and q[1]<>2 then # C(x_c,x_a)
                Add(u,(C[q[1]][2+p[2]]*C[q[1]][1+p[1]])^alpha);
            elif q[1]=1 and q[2]<>2 then # C(x_a,x_c)
                Add(u,M[1+p[1]][2+n-p[2]][q[2]]*C[1][q[2]]);
            elif q[1]=2 and q[2]<>1 then # C(x_b,x_c)
                Add(u,M[1+p[1]][2+n-p[2]][q[2]+n]*C[2][q[2]]);
            elif q=[2,1] then # C(x_b,x_a)
                Add(u,(C[2][1+p[1]]*C[1][2+p[2]])^alpha);
            else
                Add(u,C[q[1]][q[2]]);
            fi;
        od;
        for q in Arrangements([1..n],3) do for s in Tuples([0,n],3) do
            if q[2]>q[3] or s<>[0,0,0] then continue; fi;
            Add(t,M[q[1]+s[1]][q[2]+s[2]][q[3]+s[3]]);
            if q[1]+s[1]=1+p[1] and not 2 in q then # M(x_a^alpha,[x_c^gamma,x_d^delta])
                Add(u,M[q[1]+s[1]][q[2]+s[2]][q[3]+s[3]]^C[1][2+p[2]]);
            elif q[2]+s[2]=1+p[1] and not 2 in q then # M(x_c^gamma,[x_a^alpha,x_d^delta])
                Add(u,M[q[1]+s[1]][q[2]+s[2]][q[3]+s[3]]^C[q[1]][2+n-p[2]]*M[q[1]+s[1]][2+p[2]][q[3]+s[3]]);
            elif q[2]+s[2]=1+n-p[1] and not 2 in q then # M(x_c^gamma,[x_a^-alpha,x_d^delta])
                Add(u,M[q[1]+s[1]][2+n-p[2]][q[3]+s[3]]^(C[q[1]][1]^alpha)*M[q[1]+s[1]][q[2]+s[2]][q[3]+s[3]]);
            elif q[1]+s[1]=2+p[2] and not 1 in q then # M(x_b^beta,[x_c^gamma,x_d^delta])
                Add(u,(M[q[1]+s[1]][q[2]+s[2]][q[3]+s[3]]*M[1+n-p[1]][q[2]+s[2]][q[3]+s[3]])^C[1][2+p[2]]);
            elif q[1]+s[1]=2+n-p[2] and not 1 in q then # M(x_b^-beta,[x_c^gamma,x_d^delta])
                Add(u,M[2+n-p[2]][q[2]+s[2]][q[3]+s[3]]*M[1+p[1]][q[2]+s[2]][q[3]+s[3]]);
            elif q[1]+s[1]=1+p[1] and q[2]+s[2]=2+p[2] then # M(x_a^alpha,[x_b^beta,x_c^gamma])
                Add(u,M[q[1]+s[1]][q[2]+s[2]][q[3]+s[3]]^C[1][2+p[2]]);
            elif q[1]+s[1]=1+p[1] and q[2]+s[2]=2+n-p[2] then # M(x_a^alpha,[x_b^-beta,x_c^gamma])
                Add(u,M[q[1]+s[1]][q[2]+s[2]][q[3]+s[3]]^C[1][2+p[2]]);
            elif q[1]+s[1]=2+p[2] and q[2]+s[2]=1+p[1] then # M(x_b^beta,[x_a^alpha,x_c^gamma])
                Add(u,M[q[1]+n-s[1]][q[2]+s[2]][q[3]+n-s[3]]*C[2][q[3]+n-s[3]]*M[q[2]+s[2]][q[1]+n-s[1]][q[3]+s[3]]*C[1][q[3]+s[3]]);
            elif q[1]+s[1]=2+p[2] and q[2]+s[2]=1+n-p[1] then # M(x_b^beta,[x_a^-alpha,x_c^gamma])
                Add(u,(C[1][q[3]+n-s[3]]*M[1+p[1]][q[3]+s[3]][2+n-p[2]]*C[2][q[3]+s[3]]*M[2+n-p[2]][q[3]+n-s[3]][1+p[1]])^(C[q[3]][1+n-p[1]]*C[q[3]][2+n-p[2]]));
            elif q[1]+s[1]=2+n-p[2] and q[2]+s[2]=1+p[1] then # M(x_b^-beta,[x_a^alpha,x_c^gamma])
                Add(u,((C[2][q[3]+s[3]]*M[q[2]+s[2]][q[3]+s[3]][q[1]+s[1]])^C[q[3]][2+p[2]]*M[q[1]+s[1]][q[3]+s[3]][q[2]+n-s[2]])^C[q[3]][1+p[1]]*C[1][q[3]+n-s[3]]);
            elif q[1]+s[1]=2+n-p[2] and q[2]+s[2]=1+n-p[1] then # M(x_b^-beta,[x_a^-alpha,x_c^gamma])
                Add(u,(C[1][q[3]+s[3]]^C[q[3]][1+n-p[1]]*M[q[1]+s[1]][q[2]+s[2]][q[3]+s[3]])^C[q[3]][2+n-p[2]]*M[1+p[1]][q[1]+s[1]][q[3]+s[3]]*C[2][q[3]+n-s[3]]);
            elif q[2]+s[2]=1+p[1] and q[3]+s[3]=2+p[2] then # M(x_c^gamma,[x_a^alpha,x_b^beta])
                Add(u,M[q[1]+s[1]][q[2]+s[2]][q[3]+s[3]]^C[1][2+p[2]]);
            elif q[2]+s[2]=1+p[1] and q[3]+s[3]=2+n-p[2] then # M(x_c^gamma,[x_a^alpha,x_b^-beta])
                Add(u,M[q[1]+s[1]][q[3]+n-s[3]][q[2]+s[2]]);
            else
                Add(u,M[q[1]+s[1]][q[2]+s[2]][q[3]+s[3]]);
            fi;
        od; od;
        Add(endos,GroupHomomorphismByImages(F,t,u));
        u := ShallowCopy(GeneratorsOfGroup(G));
        v := ShallowCopy(GeneratorsOfGroup(G));
        if p[2]=0 then
            u[1] := (u[2]*u[1]^alpha)^alpha;
        else
            u[1] := (u[2]^-1*u[1]^alpha)^alpha;
        fi;
        Add(Gendos,GroupHomomorphismByImages(G,u));
    od;
    F := LPresentedGroup(F,[],endos,rels);
    F!.C := C; # hack: provide access to C and M generators as convenient tables
    F!.M := M;
    F!.Gendos := Gendos; # automorphisms of G such that endos[i] acts on Aut(G) by conjugation by Gendos[i]
    return GroupHomomorphismByImagesNC(F,A,epi);
end);
```

```
from pathlib import Path
p=Path('workspace/agent4_export_gap_seeds.py')
p.write_text(r'''"""Literal transcription of n=3 seed construction in GAP LPRES examples.gi.
Tuples encode words in GAP's generator ordering; GAP operation conventions retained.
"""
from itertools import permutations,product
from collections import Counter
import json
from pathlib import Path

def red(w):
 o=[]
 for x in w:
  if o and o[-1]==-x:o.pop()
  else:o.append(x)
 return tuple(o)
def mul(*ws):return red(sum(ws,()))
def inv(w):return tuple(-x for x in w[::-1])
def div(a,b):return mul(a,inv(b))
def conj(a,b):return mul(inv(b),a,b)
def comm(a,b):return mul(inv(a),inv(b),a,b)

def run(n=3):
 C={};M={};names=[]
 for i,j in permutations(range(1,n+1),2):
  names.append(('C',i,j));C[i,j]=(len(names),);C[i,j+n]=(-len(names),)
 for a,b,c in permutations(range(1,n+1),3):
  if b>c:continue
  names.append(('M',a,b,c));M[a,b,c]=(len(names),);M[a,c,b]=(-len(names),)
  M[a,c,b+n]=conj(M[a,b,c],C[a,b])
  M[a,b,c+n]=conj(M[a,c,b],C[a,c])
  M[a,c+n,b+n]=conj(M[a,b,c+n],C[a,b])
  M[a,b+n,c+n]=conj(M[a,c,b+n],C[a,c])
  M[a,c+n,b]=conj(M[a,b,c],C[a,c])
  M[a,b+n,c]=conj(M[a,c,b],C[a,b])
  for s,t in product([0,n],repeat=2):
   M[a+n,b+s,c+t]=div(comm(inv(C[a,b+s]),inv(C[a,c+t])),M[a,b+s,c+t])
   M[a+n,c+s,b+t]=div(comm(inv(C[a,c+s]),inv(C[a,b+t])),M[a,c+s,b+t])
 rel=[]
 def put(f,w):rel.append((f,red(w)))
 for a,b,c in permutations(range(1,n+1),3):
  for s,t,u in product([0,n],repeat=3):put('R0',mul(M[a+s,b+t,c+u],M[a+s,c+u,b+t]))
 for a,b in permutations(range(1,n+1),2):
  if (a,b)!=(1,2):continue
  for c,d in permutations(range(1,n+1),2):
   if a!=c and a!=d and b!=c:put('R1',comm(C[a,b],C[c,d]))
 for a,b,c in permutations(range(1,n+1),3):
  if (a,b)!=(1,2):continue
  for s,t,u in product([0,n],repeat=3):
   for d,e,f in permutations(range(1,n+1),3):
    if e>f:continue
    for v,w,x in product([0,n],repeat=3):
     if a+s!=d+v and a not in (e,f) and d not in (b,c):put('R2',comm(M[a+s,b+t,c+u],M[d+v,e+w,f+x]))
 # R3 requires four distinct labels; absent at n3.
 for a,b,c in permutations(range(1,n+1),3):
  if (a,b)!=(1,2):continue
  put('R4',comm(mul(C[c,b],C[a,b]),C[c,a]))
  for s,t,u in product([0,n],repeat=3):
   put('R5',div(conj(M[a+s,b+t,c+u],C[a,b+t]),M[a+s,c+u,b+n-t]))
   put('R6',div(mul(M[a+n-s,b+t,c+u],M[a+s,b+t,c+u]),comm(inv(C[a,b+t]),inv(C[a,c+u]))))
 # R7,R8 require four distinct labels; absent at n3.
 for a,b,c in permutations(range(1,n+1),3):
  if (a,b)!=(1,2):continue
  for d in range(1,n+1):
   if d in (a,c):continue
   for s,t,u,v in product([0,n],repeat=4):
    put('R9',div(mul(M[c+u,b+t,a+s],M[c+u,a+s,d+v],conj(M[c+u,a+s,b+t],C[c,d+n-v])),conj(M[c+u,a+s,d+v],C[a,b+t])))
 out={'convention':'GAP left-to-right map products, comm=x^-1 y^-1 x y','names':names,'rels':[{'family':f,'word':w} for f,w in rel]}
 print('all',Counter(f for f,w in rel),'nonempty',Counter(f for f,w in rel if w),'unique nonempty',len(set(w for f,w in rel if w)))
 return out,C,M
if __name__=='__main__':
 out,_,_=run();Path(__file__).with_name('agent4_gap_ia3_seeds.json').write_text(json.dumps(out,indent=2))
''')
exec(compile(p.read_text(),str(p),'exec'),{'__name__':'__main__','__file__':str(p.resolve())})

```

```
p.write_text(r'''from agent4_export_gap_seeds import *
def export():
 n=3;out,C,M=run();names=out['names'];endos=[]
 def emit(name,images):
  assert len(images)==9
  endos.append({'name':name,'images':images})
 # inversion I1
 images=[]
 for typ,*q in names:
  if typ=='C':
   a,b=q;images.append(inv(C[a,b]) if b==1 else C[a,b])
  else:
   a,b,c=q;s=[0,0,0]
   if 1 in q:s[q.index(1)]=n
   images.append(M[a+s[0],b+s[1],c+s[2]])
 emit('I1',images)
 for perm in permutations(range(1,4)):
  if perm==(1,2,3):continue
  pp={i+1:x for i,x in enumerate(perm)}
  images=[]
  for typ,*q in names:
   if typ=='C':a,b=q;images.append(C[pp[a],pp[b]])
   else:a,b,c=q;images.append(M[pp[a],pp[b],pp[c]])
  emit('P'+str(perm),images)
 for p1,p2 in product([0,n],repeat=2):
  alpha=1 if p1==0 else -1
  images=[]
  for typ,*q in names:
   if typ=='C':
    a,b=q
    if b==1 and a!=2:z=mul(C[a,2+p2],C[a,1+p1]);z=z if alpha==1 else inv(z)
    elif a==1 and b!=2:z=mul(M[1+p1,2+n-p2,b],C[1,b])
    elif a==2 and b!=1:z=mul(M[1+p1,2+n-p2,b+n],C[2,b])
    elif (a,b)==(2,1):z=mul(C[2,1+p1],C[1,2+p2]);z=z if alpha==1 else inv(z)
    else:z=C[a,b]
   else:
    a,b,c=q
    if a==1+p1 and b==2+p2:z=conj(M[a,b,c],C[1,2+p2])
    elif a==1+p1 and b==2+n-p2:z=conj(M[a,b,c],C[1,2+p2])
    elif a==2+p2 and b==1+p1:
     z=mul(M[a+n,b,c+n],C[2,c+n],M[b,a+n,c],C[1,c])
    elif a==2+p2 and b==1+n-p1:
     z=conj(mul(C[1,c+n],M[1+p1,c,2+n-p2],C[2,c],M[2+n-p2,c+n,1+p1]),mul(C[c,1+n-p1],C[c,2+n-p2]))
    elif a==2+n-p2 and b==1+p1:
     z=mul(conj(mul(conj(mul(C[2,c],M[b,c,a]),C[c,2+p2]),M[a,c,b+n]),C[c,1+p1]),C[1,c+n])
    elif a==2+n-p2 and b==1+n-p1:
     z=mul(conj(mul(conj(C[1,c],C[c,1+n-p1]),M[a,b,c]),C[c,2+n-p2]),M[1+p1,a,c],C[2,c+n])
    elif b==1+p1 and c==2+p2:z=conj(M[a,b,c],C[1,2+p2])
    elif b==1+p1 and c==2+n-p2:z=M[a,c+n,b]
    else:z=M[a,b,c]
   images.append(z)
  emit('M12'+str((p1,p2)),images)
 out['endomorphisms']=endos

if __name__=='__main__':
 out=export();Path(__file__).with_name('agent4_gap_ia3_data.json').write_text(json.dumps(out,indent=2))

''')
```

```
import sys,pickle,time
sys.path.insert(0,'workspace')
from agent2_source_relations import generators,extend,inv
from agent2_sparse_closure import UnitLattice
from agent2_gl_closure import gl_action
from itertools import combinations,product
import sympy as S

class IntegralLattice:
    def __init__(self):self.b={};self.queue=[];self.blocked=0
    def insert(self,v):
        v={i:int(c) for i,c in v.items() if c}
        changed=False
        while v:
            p=min(v);a=v[p]
            if p not in self.b:
                if a<0:v={i:-c for i,c in v.items()}
                self.b[p]=v;self.queue.append(v);return True
            w=self.b[p];d=w[p];q=a//d
            for i,c in w.items():
                t=v.get(i,0)-q*c
                if t:v[i]=t
                else:v.pop(i,None)
            if v.get(p,0):
                # Euclidean exchange: preserve old pivot row as next remainder.
                self.b[p]=v;self.queue.append(v);v=w;changed=True
        return changed

def magnus3(word,N):
    p={():1}
    for x in word:
        a=abs(x)-1; terms=[((),1),((a,),1)] if x>0 else [(tuple([a]*k),(-1)**k) for k in range(4)]
        q={}
        for u,c in p.items():
            for v,d in terms:
                if len(u)+len(v)<=3:q[u+v]=q.get(u+v,0)+c*d
        p={u:c for u,c in q.items() if c}
    return p

def compose(A,B):
    from agent2_source_relations import cat
    Ai=[inv(w) for w in A]
    return tuple(cat(*(A[x-1] if x>0 else Ai[-x-1] for x in w)) for w in B)

def cubic_sources(depth=4):
    n=3;G=generators(n);N=len(G);off=N*N
    unit=tuple((i+1,) for i in range(n));seen={unit:()};layer=[(unit,())];L=IntegralLattice(); count=0
    from agent2_source_relations import cat
    targets={};found={}
    for a in range(n):
        for b in range(n):
            if a==b:continue
            U=list(unit);Ui=list(unit);U[b]=cat(unit[b],unit[a]);Ui[b]=cat(unit[b],inv(unit[a]));U=tuple(U);Ui=tuple(Ui)
            for k,g in enumerate(G):
                target=compose(compose(U,extend(unit,g,1)),Ui)
                targets.setdefault(target,[]).append((a,b,k))
    for d in range(1,depth+1):
        nxt=[]
        for A,w in layer:

                    L.insert(v);count+=1
        layer=nxt

    print('Found Nielsen conjugate lifts',len(found),'of54; lengths',sorted(set(map(len,found.values()))),flush=True)
    pickle.dump(found,open('workspace/agent2_nielsen_lifts.pkl','wb'))

    # Full known quadratic relation lattice gives valid cubic commutator r

import sys,pickle,time
sys.path.insert(0,'workspace')
from agent2_cubic_source import IntegralLattice,magnus3
from agent2_source_relations import generators
from agent2_sparse_closure import apply
from itertools import product,combinations
import sympy as S
from sympy.matrices.normalforms import smith_normal_form
from sympy.polys.domains import ZZ

def actions():
    N=9;off=N*N;lifts=pickle.load(open('workspace/agent2_nielsen_lifts_complete.pkl','rb'));out=[]
    words=[[lifts[a,b,k] for k in range(N)] for a in range(3) for b in range(3) if a!=b]
    inverse=pickle.load(open('workspace/agent2_inversion_lifts.pkl','rb'))
    words.append([inverse[k] for k in range(N)])
    for ws in words:
        ps=[magnus3(w,N) for w in ws]
        lin=[{u:c for u,c in p.items() if len(u)==1} for p in ps]
        quad=[{u:c for u,c in p.items() if len(u)==2} for p in ps]
        act=[]
        for i,j in product(range(N),repeat=2):
            v={}
            for P,Q in [(lin[i],lin[j]),(quad[i],lin[j]),(lin[i],quad[j])]:
                for x,c in P.items():
                    for y,d in Q.items():
                        w=x+y;k=w[0]*N+w[1] if len(w)==2 else off+(w[0]*N+w[1])*N+w[2]
                        v[k]=v.get(k,0)+c*d
            act.append({k:c for k,c in v.items() if c})
        for i,j,k in product(range(N),repeat=3):
            v={}
            for x,c in lin[i].items():
                for y,d in lin[j].items():
                    for z,e in lin[k].items():v[off+(x[0]*N+y[0])*N+z[0]]=c*d*e
            act.append(v)
        out.append(act)
    return out

def run():
    start=time.time();B=pickle.load(open('workspace/agent2_ia3_mixed_relations.pkl','rb'));L=IntegralLattice()
    for v in B.values():L.insert(v)
    H=pickle.load(open('workspace/agent2_ia3_GL_relations.pkl','rb'));N=9;off=81;P=list(combinations(range(N),2))
    for col in range(H.cols):
        p={}
        for r,(i,j) in enumerate(P):
            c=int(H[r,col])
            if c:p[(i,j)]=c;p[(j,i)]=-c
        for k in range(N):
            v={}
            for (i,j),c in p.items():
                a=off+(i*N+j)*N+k;b=off+(k*N+i)*N+j
                v[a]=v.get(a,0)+c;v[b]=v.get(b,0)-c
            L.insert(v)
    acts=actions();idx=0
    while idx<len(L.queue):
        v=L.queue[idx];idx+=1
        for act in acts:L.insert(apply(act,v))
    C={p-off:{i-off:c for i,c in v.items()} for p,v in L.b.items() if p>=off}
    print('Mixed relation rank',len(L.b),'pure cubic rank',len(C),'time',time.time()-start,flush=True)
    M=S.zeros(729,len(C))
    for j,v in enumerate(C.values()):
        for i,c in v.items():M[i,j]=c
    D=smith_normal_form(M,domain=ZZ);ds=[abs(D[i,i]) for i in range(len(C))]
    from collections import Counter
    print('SNF',dict(Counter(ds)),flush=True)
    assert len(C)==197 and all(d==1 for d in ds)
    pickle.dump(C,open('workspace/agent2_ia3_cubic_primitive_relations.pkl','wb'))
    return C
if __name__=='__main__':run()
```

```
from itertools import combinations
from collections import Counter
from math import gcd
import sympy as S
from sympy.matrices.normalforms import smith_normal_form, hermite_normal_form
from sympy.polys.domains import ZZ

def red(w):
    st=[]
    for x in w:
        if st and st[-1]==-x:st.pop()
        else:st.append(x)
    return tuple(st)
def inv(w):return tuple(-i for i in w[::-1])
def cat(*words):return red(x for w in words for x in w)
def cm(a,b):return cat(a,b,inv(a),inv(b))
def generators(n):
    out=[]
    for i in range(n):
        for j in range(n):
            if i!=j:out.append(('C',i,j))
        for j,k in combinations(range(n),2):
            if i not in (j,k):out.append(('M',i,j,k))
    return out

def extend(A,g,s):
    typ,i,*args=g
    B=list(A)
    if typ=='C':
        j=args[0]; v=A[j] if s==1 else inv(A[j]);B[i]=cat(v,A[i],inv(v))
    else:
        j,k=args;v=cm(A[j],A[k]);B[i]=cat(A[i],v if s==1 else inv(v))
    return tuple(B)

def lattice_relations(n=3,depth=4):
    G=generators(n);N=len(G)
    pairs=list(combinations(range(N),2));pidx={p:i for i,p in enumerate(pairs)};M=len(pairs)
    unit=tuple((i+1,) for i in range(n))
    z=(0,)*N; zz=(0,)*M
    seen={unit:(z,zz,())}; layer=[(unit,z,zz,())]
    rels={}; found=Counter()
    for d in range(1,depth+1):
        nxt=[]
        for A,e,c,w in layer:
            for a,g in enumerate(G):
                for s in (1,-1):
                    letter=s*(a+1)
                    if w and w[-1]==-letter:continue
                    B=extend(A,g,s)
                    f=list(e); f[a]+=s; f=tuple(f)
                    v=list(c)
                    for j in range(a+1,N):v[pidx[a,j]]-=s*e[j]
                    v=tuple(v); ww=w+(letter,)
                    if B in seen:
                        f0,v0,w0=seen[B]
                        assert f0==f
                        rel=tuple(x-y for x,y in zip(v,v0))
                        if any(rel):
                            if next(x for x in rel if x)<0:rel=tuple(-x for x in rel); ww,w0=w0,ww
                            if rel not in rels:rels[rel]=(ww,w0);found[d]+=1
                    else:
                        seen[B]=(f,v,ww);nxt.append((B,f,v,ww))
        layer=nxt
        R=S.Matrix(list(rels)).T if rels else S.zeros(M,0)
        print('depth',d,'seen',len(seen),'layer',len(layer),'relations',len(rels),'rank',R.rank(),flush=True)
    R=S.Matrix(list(rels)).T
    H=hermite_normal_form(R)
    D=smith_normal_form(H,domain=ZZ)
    elementary=[D[i,i] for i in range(min(D.shape))]
    print('relation rank',H.cols,'Smith invariants',elementary,flush=True)
    return G,pairs,rels,H

if __name__=='__main__':
    import pickle
    G,pairs,rels,H=lattice_relations(depth=5)
    with open('workspace/agent2_ia3_quadratic_relations.pkl','wb') as f:pickle.dump((G,pairs,rels,H),f)
```

```
FILE gap/pkg/lpres/README.md 
 [![CI](https://github.com/gap-packages/lpres/actions/workflows/CI.yml/badge.svg)](https://github.com/gap-packages/lpres/actions/workflows/CI.yml)
[![Code Coverage](https://codecov.io/github/gap-packages/lpres/coverage.svg?branch=master&token=)](https://codecov.io/gh/gap-packages/lpres)

# The GAP 4 package `lpres`

## Introduction

This is the package `lpres` written for GAP 4. It provides a first
construction of finitely L-presented groups and a nilpotent quotient
algorithm for L-presented groups.

The features of this package include

  - creating an L-presented group as new gap object,

  - computing nilpotent quotients of L-presented groups and epimorphisms
    from the L-presented group onto its nilpotent quotients,

  - computing the abelian invariants of an L-presented group,

  - computing finite-index subgroups and if possible their L-presentation

  - approximating the Schur multiplier of L-presented groups.

There is a manual in the subdirectory `doc` written in plain TeX which
describes the functions available.

If you have found a bug or any features missing please let me know
(Laurent Bartholdi, laurent.bartholdi@gmail.com)


## Contents

With this version you should have obtained the following files and
directories:

    README.lpres    this file
    
    init.g          the file that initializes this package
    
    read.g          the file that reads in the package        
    
    makedoc.g       the file used to compile the documentation
    
    PackageInfo.g   the file for the new package loading mechanism
    
    doc             the manual
    
    gap             the GAP code


## Installation

Make sure that the GAP 4 packages Polycyclic and FGA are installed. It
suffices to unpack the package in the `pkg` directory and load the
package from within gap using `LoadPackage("lpres");`.


## Test Files

The lpres package can be tested with

    ReadPackage("lpres","tst/testall.g");


## Compiling the Manual

If you obtained the package from its git repository, you have to compile
the manual. For this, enter the directory of lpres (the one containing
the file `makedoc.g`) and run `gap makedoc.g`.

## BugFixes/Changes/Features Added

* Version 0.1.0:
  - maintenance taken over by Laurent Bartholdi

FILE gap/pkg/lpres/gap/examples.gi 
 ############################################################################
##
#W examples.gi			LPRES				René Hartung
##

############################################################################
##
#F  ExamplesOfLPresentations ( <int> )
##
## returns some important examples of L-presented groups (e.g. Grigorchuk).
##
InstallGlobalFunction(ExamplesOfLPresentations,
  function( n )
  local F,F2,		# free group
        rels,		# fixed relators
        sigma,tau,	# endomorphism
        endos,		# set of endomorphisms
        itrels,		# iterated relators
        a,b,c,d,	# free group generators
        t,r,u,v, 	# free group generators
        T,U,V, 		# free group generators
        G,		# L-presented group
	e,f,g,h,i,	# for abbreviation in the Hanoi tower group
	IL;		# info level of InfoLPRES


  if n=1 then 
    # The Grigorchuk group on 4 generators
    Info(InfoLPRES,1,"The Grigorchuk group on 4 generators from [Lys85]");
    F:=FreeGroup("a","b","c","d");
    a:=F.1;;b:=F.2;;c:=F.3;;d:=F.4;;
    rels:=[a^2,b^2,c^2,d^2,b*c*d];
    sigma:=GroupHomomorphismByImagesNC(F,F,[a,b,c,d],[c^a,d,b,c]);
    endos:=[sigma];
    itrels:=[Comm(d,d^a),Comm(d,d^(a*c*a*c*a))];
    
    G:=LPresentedGroup(F,rels,endos,itrels);
    SetIsInvariantLPresentation(G,true);
    SetSize( G, infinity );
  elif n=2 then
    # The Grigorchuk group on 3 generators
    Info(InfoLPRES,1,"The Grigorchuk group on 3 generators");
    F:=FreeGroup("a","c","d");
    a:=F.1;;c:=F.2;;d:=F.3;;
    rels:=[];
    sigma:=GroupHomomorphismByImagesNC(F,F,[a,c,d],[c^a,c*d,c]);
    endos:=[sigma];
    itrels:=[a^2,Comm(d,d^a),Comm(d,d^(a*c*a*c*a))];
  
    G:=LPresentedGroup(F,rels,endos,itrels);
    SetSize( G, infinity );
  elif n=3 then 
    # The lamplighter group \Z_2 \wr \Z
    Info(InfoLPRES,1,"The lamplighter group on two lamp states");
    IL := InfoLevel( InfoLPRES );
    SetInfoLevel( InfoLPRES, 0 );
    G := LamplighterGroup( IsLpGroup, 2 );
    SetInfoLevel( InfoLPRES, IL );
    SetSize( G, infinity );
  elif n=4 then 
    # Brunner-Sidki-Vieira group
    Info(InfoLPRES,1,"The Brunner-Sidki-Vieira group");
    F:=FreeGroup("a","b");
    a:=F.1;;b:=F.2;;
    rels:=[];
    sigma:=GroupHomomorphismByImagesNC(F,F,[a,b],[b^2*a^(-1)*b^2,b^2]);
    endos:=[sigma];
    itrels:=[Comm(a,a^b),Comm(a,a^(b^3))];
  
    G:=LPresentedGroup(F,rels,endos,itrels);
    SetSize( G, infinity );
  elif n=5 then 
    # The Grigorchuk supergroup
    Info(InfoLPRES,1,"The Grigorchuk supergroup");
    F:=FreeGroup("a","b","c","d");
    a:=F.1;;b:=F.2;;c:=F.3;;d:=F.4;;
    rels:=[];
    sigma:=GroupHomomorphismByImagesNC(F,F,[a,b,c,d],[a*b*a,d,b,c]);
    endos:=[sigma];
    itrels:=[a^2,Comm(b,c),Comm(c,c^a),Comm(c,d^a),Comm(d,d^a),Comm(c^(a*b),
             (c^(a*b))^a),Comm(c^(a*b),(d^(a*b))^a),Comm(d^(a*b),(d^(a*b))^a)];
  
    G:=LPresentedGroup(F,rels,endos,itrels);
    SetSize( G, infinity );
  elif n=6 then 
    # The Fabrykowski-Gupta group
    Info(InfoLPRES,1,"The Fabrykowski-Gupta group");
    G:=GeneralizedFabrykowskiGuptaLpGroup( 3 );
    SetSize( G, infinity );
  elif n=7 then 
    # The Gupta-Sidki group
    Info(InfoLPRES,1,"The Gupta-Sidki group");
    F:=FreeGroup("a","t","u","v");
    a:=F.1;;t:=F.2;;   u:=F.3;;   v:=F.4;;
            T:=F.2^-1;;U:=F.3^-1;;V:=F.4^-1;;

    rels:=[a^3,t^3,t^a/u,t^(a^2)/v];

    itrels:=[ u*T*v*T*U*V*t*v*U*t*U*V*T*u*T*u*v*t*U*t*V*U*v*t*u*V*u*T, 
            u*T*v*T*U*V*T*U*v*U*T*V*u*T*u*v*t*U*t*V*u, 
            u*T*v*T*U*V*t*U*T*u*v*t*U*t*V*U*t*v*u*T*u*V, 
            v*T*u*T*V*U*t*v*U*t*U*V*T*u*t*U*v*t*u*V*u*T, 
            v*T*u*T*V*U*T*U*v*U*T*V*u*t*u, v*T*u*T*V*U*t*U*t*U*t*v*u*T*u*V, 
            T*v*U*t*U*V*T*u*T*v*u*t*V*t*u*v*t*u*V*u*T, U*v*U*T*V*u*T*v*u*t*V*t, 
 	    T*U*T*v*u*t*V*t*u*t*v*u*T*u*V, u*T*v*T*U*V*T*V*u*V*T*U*v*t*v, 
            u*T*v*T*U*V*t*u*V*t*V*U*T*v*t*V*u*t*v*U*v*T, 
            u*T*v*T*U*V*t*V*t*V*t*u*v*T*v*U,
            v*T*u*T*V*U*T*V*u*V*T*U*v*T*v*u*t*V*t*U*v, 
            v*T*u*T*V*U*t*u*V*t*V*U*T*v*T*v*u*t*V*t*U*V*u*t*v*U*v*T, 
            v*T*u*T*V*U*t*V*T*v*u*t*V*t*U*V*t*u*v*T*v*U, 
            V*u*V*T*U*v*T*u*v*t*U*t, T*V*T*u*v*t*U*t*v*t*u*v*T*v*U,
```

```
import json,sys,pickle,time
sys.path.insert(0,'workspace')
from agent2_cubic_source import magnus3,IntegralLattice
from agent2_sparse_closure import apply
from itertools import product
import sympy as S
from sympy.matrices.normalforms import smith_normal_form
from sympy.polys.domains import ZZ
from collections import Counter
N=9;off=81;data=json.load(open('workspace/agent4_gap_ia3_data.json'))
def vector(p):
    v={}
    for u,c in p.items():
        if len(u)==2:v[u[0]*N+u[1]]=c
        if len(u)==3:v[off+(u[0]*N+u[1])*N+u[2]]=c
    return v

def action(ws):
    ps=[magnus3(tuple(w[::-1]),N) for w in ws]
    lin=[{u:c for u,c in p.items() if len(u)==1} for p in ps]
    quad=[{u:c for u,c in p.items() if len(u)==2} for p in ps]
    act=[]
    for i,j in product(range(N),repeat=2):
        p={}
        for P,Q in [(lin[i],lin[j]),(quad[i],lin[j]),(lin[i],quad[j])]:
            for x,c in P.items():
                for y,d in Q.items():p[x+y]=p.get(x+y,0)+c*d
        act.append(vector(p))
    for i,j,k in product(range(N),repeat=3):
        p={}
        for x,c in lin[i].items():
            for y,d in lin[j].items():
                for z,e in lin[k].items():p[x+y+z]=p.get(x+y+z,0)+c*d*e
        act.append(vector(p))
    return act

def comm(v,k):
    ans={}
    for i,c in v.items():
        if i>=off:continue
        a,b=divmod(i,N);j=off+(a*N+b)*N+k;l=off+(k*N+a)*N+b
        ans[j]=ans.get(j,0)+c;ans[l]=ans.get(l,0)-c
    return ans
start=time.time();L=IntegralLattice()
for r in data['rels']:
    p=magnus3(tuple(r['word'][::-1]),N)
    assert not any(len(u)==1 for u in p)
    L.insert(vector(p))
acts=[action(e['images']) for e in data['endomorphisms']];idx=0
while idx<len(L.queue):
    v=L.queue[idx];idx+=1
    for k in range(N):L.insert(comm(v,k))
    for act in acts:L.insert(apply(act,v))
for iteration in range(10):
    changed=0
    for v in list(L.b.values()):
        for k in range(N):changed+=L.insert(comm(v,k))
        for act in acts:changed+=L.insert(apply(act,v))
    print('stability',iteration,'changes',changed,flush=True)
    if not changed:break
C={p-off:{i-off:c for i,c in v.items()} for p,v in L.b.items() if p>=off}
M=S.zeros(729,len(C))
for j,v in enumerate(C.values()):
    for i,c in v.items():M[i,j]=c
D=smith_normal_form(M,domain=ZZ);ds=[abs(D[i,i]) for i in range(len(C))]
print('Direct LPRES mixed rank',len(L.b),'pure cubic rank',len(C),'SNF',dict(Counter(ds)),'secs',time.time()-start,flush=True)
pickle.dump(C,open('workspace/agent2_direct_lpres_cubic.pkl','wb'))
```

```
def apply(act,v):
    ans={}
    for i,c in v.items():
        for j,d in act[i].items():
            t=ans.get(j,0)+c*d
            if t:ans[j]=t
            else:ans.pop(j,None)
    return ans

def seed(n,L):
    G=generators(n);gi={g:i for i,g in enumerate(G)};P=list(combinations(range(len(G)),2));pi={p:i for i,p in enumerate(P)}
    unit=tuple((i+1,) for i in range(n))
    for k,(i,j) in enumerate(P):
        if extend(extend(unit,G[i],1),G[j],1)==extend(extend(unit,G[j],1),G[i],1):L.insert({k:1})
    print('commuting rank',len(L.b),flush=True)
    G4,P4,R4,H4=pickle.load(open('workspace/agent2_ia4_quadratic_relations.pkl','rb'))
    H4=pickle.load(open('workspace/agent2_ia4_GL_relations.pkl','rb'))
    cols=[{i:int(H4[i,j]) for i in range(H4.rows) if H4[i,j]} for j in range(H4.cols)]
    for subset in combinations(range(n),4):
        ids=[]
        for g in G4:ids.append(gi[(g[0],)+tuple(subset[x] for x in g[1:])])
        emb={i:(pi[min(ids[a],ids[b]),max(ids[a],ids[b])],1 if ids[a]<ids[b] else -1) for i,(a,b) in enumerate(P4)}
        for col in cols:L.insert({emb[i][0]:c*emb[i][1] for i,c in col.items()})
    print('seed rank',len(L.b),'blocked',L.blocked,flush=True)
```

```
Path('workspace/agent3_aut_action_loops.py').write_text(Path('scratchpad.py').read_text())

def inv(w):return tuple(-i for i in w[::-1])
def reduce(w):
 out=[]
 for i in w:
  if out and out[-1]==-i:out.pop()
  else:out.append(i)
 return tuple(out)
def substitute(w,aut):
 return reduce(j for i in w for j in (aut[i-1] if i>0 else inv(aut[-i-1])))
def compose(a,b):return tuple(substitute(w,a) for w in b)
identity=((1,),(2,),(3,))
from itertools import permutations
ags=[((-1,),(2,),(3,))]
for p in permutations((1,2,3)):
 if p!=(1,2,3):ags.append(tuple((i,) for i in p))
ags += [((2,1),(2,),(3,)),((-2,1),(2,),(3,)),((1,-2),(2,),(3,)),((1,2),(2,),(3,))]
assert len(ags)==len(x['endomorphisms'])
seen={identity:()};front=[identity];loops=[];loopseen=set()
for radius in range(1,5):
 nxt=[]
 for old in front:
  w=seen[old]
  for j,g in enumerate(ags):
   a=compose(g,old);v=w+(j,)
   if a not in seen:
    seen[a]=v;nxt.append(a)
   elif seen[a]!=v:
    pair=(v,seen[a])
    if pair not in loopseen:loopseen.add(pair);loops.append(pair)
 front=nxt
 print('radius',radius,'seen',len(seen),'new',len(front),'loops',len(loops),flush=True)
Path('workspace/agent3_aut_action_loops.json').write_text(json.dumps({'endo_names':[e['name'] for e in x['endomorphisms']], 'loops':loops,'convention':'word actions append means compose new g on left: Sg o old. Both sides have equal actual AutF3 images.'}))
```

```
"""Fast field-membership tests for current VERIFIED rank3 source relation lattice.
Source generators: C12,C13,C21,C23,C31,C32,M1;23,M2;13,M3;12.
M is LEFT multiplication xi -> [xj,xk]xi. Word products R-to-L composition.
Each returned nonzero residue certifies an additional source relation is new
IF supplied word has independently been checked an actual automorphism identity.
This is NOT an IA presentation or a classification theorem.
"""
import pickle,sys
sys.path.insert(0,'workspace')
from agent2_cubic_source import magnus3

def insert(B,v,p):
    v={k:int(c)%p for k,c in v.items() if c%p}
    while v:
        k=min(v);c=v[k]
        if k in B:
            for j,d in B[k].items():
                a=(v.get(j,0)-c*d)%p
                if a:v[j]=a
                else:v.pop(j,None)
        else:
            a=pow(c,-1,p);B[k]={j:a*d%p for j,d in v.items()};return True
    return False

def reduce(B,v,p):
    v={k:int(c)%p for k,c in v.items() if c%p}
    # Keep nonpivot terms; cannot stop at the first such term.
    for k,w in sorted(B.items()):
        c=v.get(k,0)
        if c:
            for j,d in w.items():
                a=(v.get(j,0)-c*d)%p
                if a:v[j]=a
                else:v.pop(j,None)
    return v

def vector(word):
    p=magnus3(tuple(word),9);v={}
    assert not any(len(u)==1 for u in p),'identity word must have zero exponent sums'
    for u,c in p.items():
        if len(u)==2:v[9*u[0]+u[1]]=c
        if len(u)==3:v[81+81*u[0]+9*u[1]+u[2]]=c
    return v

def load():return pickle.load(open('workspace/agent2_source_defect_bases.pkl','rb'))
def defect(word,bases=None):
    if bases is None:bases=load()
    v=vector(word)
    return {p:reduce(B,v,p) for p,B in bases.items()}
```

```
--- stdout ---
#############################################################################
##
#W  AutGrp.gi                FGA package                    Christian Sievers
##
##  Methods for automorphism groups of free groups
##
#Y  2003 - 2012
##


#############################################################################
##
#M  AutomorphismGroup( <group> )
##
InstallMethod( AutomorphismGroup,
    "for free groups",
    [ CanComputeWithInverseAutomaton ],
    function( G )
    local n, aut;

    n := RankOfFreeGroup( G );

    if n = 0 then

        aut := AsGroup( [ IdentityMapping ( G ) ] );

    elif n = 1 then

        aut := Group( FreeGroupAutomorphismsGeneratorO( G ) );
        SetSize( aut, 2 );

    elif n = 2 then

        aut := Group( FreeGroupAutomorphismsGeneratorO( G ),
                      FreeGroupAutomorphismsGeneratorP( G ), 
                      FreeGroupAutomorphismsGeneratorU( G ) );

    elif n = 3 then

        aut := Group( FreeGroupAutomorphismsGeneratorS( G ),
                      FreeGroupAutomorphismsGeneratorT( G ),
                      FreeGroupAutomorphismsGeneratorU( G ) );

    elif IsEvenInt( n ) then

        aut := Group( FreeGroupAutomorphismsGeneratorQ( G ),
                      FreeGroupAutomorphismsGeneratorR( G ) );

    else  #  n > 3, odd

        aut := Group( FreeGroupAutomorphismsGeneratorS( G ),
                      FreeGroupAutomorphismsGeneratorR( G ) );

    fi;

    SetIsFinite( aut, n <= 1 );
    SetFilterObj( aut, IsAutomorphismGroupOfFreeGroup );

    return aut;

    end );

#############################################################################
##
#M  \in( <hom>, <autgrp> )
##
## tests whether <hom> is in <autgrp>.
##
## Code contributed by Max Horn.
 
InstallMethod( \in,
    "for automorphism groups of free groups",
    [ IsGroupGeneralMapping, IsAutomorphismGroupOfFreeGroup ],
    function( hom, aut )
        local G;
        G := AutomorphismDomain( aut );
        return Source( hom ) = G and Range( hom ) = G and IsBijective( hom );
    end );

#############################################################################
##
#M  IsomorphismFpGroup( <group> )
##
##  returns an isomorphism from an automorphism group of a free group
##  to a finitely presented group.
##
##  The presentation follows Bernhard Neumann (see ../doc/manual.bib)
##  Numbers in the comments refer to the equation numbers in that paper.
##
InstallMethod( IsomorphismFpGroup,
    "for automorphism groups of free groups",
    [ IsAutomorphismGroupOfFreeGroup ],
    function( aut )

    local n, f, fp, O, P, U, S, T, Q, R, rels, moreRels,
                    o, p, u, s, t, q, r, iso, isoinv;

    n := RankOfFreeGroup( AutomorphismDomain ( aut ));

    if n = 0 then

        fp := FreeGroup( 0 );

        iso := aut -> One( fp );

    elif n = 1 then

        f := FreeGroup( "O" );
        O := f.1;
        rels := [ O^2 ];  # 6a
        fp := f / rels;

        o := fp.1;
        iso := aut -> o ^ (Order(aut) - 1);

    elif n = 2 then

        f := FreeGroup( "O", "P", "U" );
        O := f.1;  P := f.2;  U := f.3;
        rels := [ P^2                # 5a
                , O^2                # 6a
                , (O*P)^4            # 8a
                , Comm( U, O*U*O )   # 7e
                , (P*O*P*U)^2        # 7i
                , (U*P*O)^3          # 8b
                ];
        fp := f / rels;

        o := fp.1;  p := fp.2;  u := fp.3;
        iso := FGA_CurryAutToPQOU( p, p, o, u); # Q=P

    elif n = 3 then

        f := FreeGroup( "S", "T", "U" );
        S := f.1;  T := f.2;  U := f.3;
        rels := [ (S^5*T^-1)^2                     # 19a
                , T^-1*S*T^2*S^8*T^-1*S*T^2*S^-4   # 19b
                , (S^4*T^-1*S*T^-1)^2              # 19c
                , T^4                              # 19d
                , Comm( U, S^2*T^-1*S*T^-1*S^2 )   # 19e
                , Comm( U, S^-2*T^-1*S*T^-1*U*S^-2*T^-1*S*T^-1) # 19f
        # wrong:  Comm( U, S^2*T^-1*S*T*S*T^-1*U*T*S^-1*T^-1*S^-1*T*S^2 ) # 19g
                , Comm( U, S^-2*T^-1*S*T*S*T^-1*U*
                              T*S^-1*T^-1*S^-1*T*S^2 ) # 19g corrected
                , Comm( U, T^-1*S*T^2*U*T^-1*S*T^2 )   # 19h
                , S^-2*T^-1*S*T^2*S^2*U*S^2*T^-1*S*T^2*S^2*
                  U*S^-2*U*S^2*U^-1*S^-2*U^-1               # 19i
                , (S^-2*T^-1*S*T*U)^2              # 19j
                , (U*T)^3                          # 19k
                ];
        fp := f / rels;

        s := fp.1;  t := fp.2;  u := fp.3;
        iso := FGA_CurryAutToPQOU( t*s^3*(s*t^-1)^2  # 16c
                                 , s^4               # 16a
                                 , s^3*(s*t^-1)^2    # 16b
                                 , u
                                 );

    elif IsEvenInt(n) then

        f := FreeGroup( "Q", "R" );
        Q := f.1;  R := f.2;
        rels := [ (R^3*(Q*R^3)^(n-1))^2            # 22a
                , (Q*R^3)^(2*(n-1))                # 22d
                , Q^n                              # 22e
                , Comm( (Q*R^3)^(n-1),
                        Q^-1*R^3*(Q*R^3)^(n-1)*Q ) # 22f
                , Comm( Q^-2*R^4*Q^2*R^-3,
                        Q*R^-3*Q^-1*R^-3*Q )       # 22h
                , Comm( R^4, (Q*R^3)^(n-1) )       # 22j
                , Comm( R^4, Q^-2*R^4*Q^2 )        # 22k
                , Comm( Q^-2*R^4*Q^2*R^-3,
                        (Q*R^3)^(n-1)*Q^-2*R^4*Q^2*R^-3*(Q*R^3)^(n-1) )  # 22l
                , Comm( Q^-2*R^4*Q^2*R^-3,
                        R^-3*Q^-1*Q^-2*R^4*Q^2*R^-3*Q*R^3 )  # 22m
                , Comm( Q^-2*R^4*Q^2*R^-3,
                        R^-3*Q^-1*R^-3*(Q*R^3)^n*Q^-2*R^4*
                          Q^2*R^-3*(Q*R^3)^-n*R^3*Q*R^3   )  # 22n
                , (Q*R^3)^-n*Q^-2*R^4*Q^2*R^-3*(Q*R^3)^n*
                    Q^-3*R^4*Q^2*R^-3*Q^-1*R^4*Q^2*R^-3*
                    Q^-1*R^3*Q^-2*R^-4*Q^3*R^3*Q^-2*
... (1,817 characters cut) ...
2*R^-3,
#                       R^-3*S^-1*R^-3*S*R^3*S^n*
#                         (S*R^-3)^(n-1)*S^-2*R^4*S^2*R^3*
#                         (S*R^-3)^(n-1)*S^n*R^-3*S^-1*
#                         R^3*S*R^3                       )  # 25k
                , Comm( S^-2*R^4*S^2*R^-3,
                        R^-3*S^-1*R^-3*S*R^3*S^n*
                          (S*R^-3)^(n-1)*S^-2*R^4*S^2*R^-3*
                          (S*R^-3)^(n-1)*S^n*R^-3*S^-1*
                          R^3*S*R^3                       )  # 25k corrected

                , R^3*(S*R^-3)^(n-1)*S^-3*R^4*S^2*R^-3*S*
                    R^3*(S*R^-3)^(n-1)*S^(n-3)*R^4*S^2*R^-3*
                    S^(n-1)*R^4*S^2*R^-3*S^(n-1)*R^3*S^-2*
                    R^-4*S^(n+3)*R^3*S^-2*R^-4*S^2           # 25l
                , (R^3*(S*R^-3)^(n-1)*S^(n-2)*R^4*S^2)^2     # 25m
                , R^12                                       # 22q
                ] ;
        # and finally the relations 25c:
        moreRels := List( [ 2 .. (n-1)/2 ],
                          i -> Comm( R^3*S^n*(S*R^-3)^(n-1),
                                     S^(-i*(n+1))*R^3*S^n*
                                     (S*R^-3)^(n-1)*S^(i*(n+1)) ) );
        fp := f / Concatenation( rels, moreRels );

        s := fp.1;  r := fp.2;
        iso := FGA_CurryAutToPQOU( r^3*s^n*(s*r^-3)^(n-1)              # 24c
                                 , s^(n+1)                             # 24a
                                 , s^n*(s*r^-3)^(n-1)                  # 24b
                                 , s^(-2*(n+1))*r^4*s^(2*(n+1))*r^-3   # 24d
                                 );

    fi;

    isoinv := GroupHomomorphismByImagesNC( fp, aut,
                                           GeneratorsOfGroup( fp ),
                                           GeneratorsOfGroup( aut ) );

    return GroupHomomorphismByFunction( aut, fp, iso,
                                        x -> x ^ isoinv );

    end );


#############################################################################
##
#F  FreeGroupEndomorphismByImages( <group>, <images> )
##
##  returns the endomorphism of <group> that maps the generators of <group>
##  to <images>.
##
InstallGlobalFunction( FreeGroupEndomorphismByImages,
    function(g,l)
    return GroupHomomorphismByImages(g,g,FreeGeneratorsOfGroup(g),l);
    end );


#############################################################################
##
#F  FreeGroupAutomorphismsGeneratorO( <group> )
#F  FreeGroupAutomorphismsGeneratorP( <group> )
#F  FreeGroupAutomorphismsGeneratorU( <group> )
#F  FreeGroupAutomorphismsGeneratorS( <group> )
#F  FreeGroupAutomorphismsGeneratorT( <group> )
#F  FreeGroupAutomorphismsGeneratorQ( <group> )
#F  FreeGroupAutomorphismsGeneratorR( <group> )
##
##  These functions return the automorphism of <group> which maps the
##  generators [<x1>, <x2>, ..., <xn>] to
##  O : [<x1>^-1 , <x2>,       ..., <xn>   ]        (n>=1)
##  P : [<x2>    , <x1>, <x3>, ..., <xn>   ]        (n>=2)
##  U : [<x1><x2>, <x2>, <x3>, ..., <xn>   ]        (n>=2)
##  S : [<x2>^-1, <x3>^-1, ..., <xn>^-1, <x1>^-1 ]  (n>=1)
##  T : [<x2>    , <x1>^-1, <x3>, ..., <xn>]        (n>=2)
##  Q : [<x2>, <x3>, ..., <xn>, <x1> ]              (n>=2)
##  R : [<x2>^-1, <x1>, <x3>, <x4>, ..., 
##       <x{n-2}>, <xn><x{n-1}>^-1, <x{n-1}>^-1]    (n>=4)
##
InstallGlobalFunction( FreeGroupAutomorphismsGeneratorO,
    function( g )
    local imgs;
    FGA_CheckRank( g, 1 );
    imgs := ShallowCopy( FreeGeneratorsOfGroup( g ) );
    imgs[1] := imgs[1]^-1;
    return FreeGroupEndomorphismByImages( g, imgs );
    end );

InstallGlobalFunction( FreeGroupAutomorphismsGeneratorP,
    function( g )
    local imgs;
    FGA_CheckRank( g, 2 );
    imgs := ShallowCopy( FreeGeneratorsOfGroup( g ) );
    imgs{[1,2]} := [ imgs[2], imgs[1] ];
    return FreeGroupEndomorphismByImages( g, imgs );
    end );

InstallGlobalFunction( FreeGroupAutomorphismsGeneratorU,
    function( g )
    local imgs;
    imgs := ShallowCopy( FreeGeneratorsOfGroup( g ) );
    FGA_CheckRank( g, 2 );
    imgs[1] := imgs[1] * imgs[2];
    return FreeGroupEndomorphismByImages( g, imgs );
    end );

InstallGlobalFunction( FreeGroupAutomorphismsGeneratorS,
    function( g )
    local imgs;
    FGA_CheckRank( g, 1 );
    imgs := FreeGeneratorsOfGroup(g){[2..Rank(g)]};
    Add( imgs, FreeGeneratorsOfGroup(g)[1] );
    return FreeGroupEndomorphismByImages( g, List(imgs, g -> g^-1) );
    end );

InstallGlobalFunction( FreeGroupAutomorphismsGeneratorT,
    function( g )
    local imgs;
    FGA_CheckRank( g, 2 );
    imgs := ShallowCopy( FreeGeneratorsOfGroup( g ) );
    imgs{[1..2]} := [ imgs[2], imgs[1]^-1 ];
    return FreeGroupEndomorphismByImages( g, imgs );
    end );

InstallGlobalFunction( FreeGroupAutomorphismsGeneratorQ,
    function( g )
    local imgs;
    FGA_CheckRank( g, 2 ); # we could allow 1
    imgs := FreeGeneratorsOfGroup(g){[2..Rank(g)]};
    Add( imgs, FreeGeneratorsOfGroup(g)[1] );
    return FreeGroupEndomorphismByImages( g, imgs );
    end );

InstallGlobalFunction( FreeGroupAutomorphismsGeneratorR,
    function( g )
    local imgs, n;
    FGA_CheckRank( g, 4 );
    n := RankOfFreeGroup( g );
    imgs := ShallowCopy( FreeGeneratorsOfGroup( g ) );
    imgs{[1,2,n-1,n]} := [ imgs[2]^-1,           imgs[1],
                           imgs[n]*imgs[n-1]^-1, imgs[n-1]^-1 ];
    return FreeGroupEndomorphismByImages( g, imgs );
    end );

#############################################################################
##
#F FGA_CheckRank( <group>, <minrank> )
##
## Checks whether <group> has rank at least <minrank>, and signals an
## error otherwise (helper function for FreeGroupAutomorphismsGenerator*)
##
InstallGlobalFunction( FGA_CheckRank,
    function( g, r )
    if RankOfFreeGroup( g ) < r then
        Error( "the rank of the group should be at least ", r );
    fi;
    return; 
    end );

#############################################################################
##
#E
```

```
print('K2 exactfp',len(gap.GeneratorsOfGroup(Kfp)),len(gap.RelatorsOfFpGroup(Kfp)),time.time()-t,flush=True)

(base/'agent4_K2_fp_data.json').write_text(json.dumps(D,default=int))
# Build short known STU words for all ten standard Nielsen generators.
O=(1,1,1,1,-2,1,-2);PP=mul((2,),O);QQ=(1,1,1,1)
Pmap=((2,),(1,),(3,));Qmap=((2,),(3,),(1,))
seen={identity:()};front=[identity]
while front:
 nxt=[]
 for aa in front:
  for bb,ww in [(Pmap,PP),(Qmap,QQ)]:
   cc=compose(bb,aa)
   if cc not in seen:seen[cc]=mul(seen[aa],ww);nxt.append(cc)
 front=nxt
from itertools import permutations
NW=[O]+[seen[tuple((i,) for i in perm)] for perm in permutations((1,2,3)) if perm!=(1,2,3)]+[mul(O,(-3,),O),mul(O,(3,),O),(-3,),(3,)]
# Convert explicit short STU words to fp Aut words, then exact K2 generators.
pg=list(gap.GeneratorsOfGroup(P))
def evalfp(w):
 z=gap.One(P)
 for x in w:z=z*(pg[x-1] if x>0 else pg[-x-1]**-1)
 return z
IAwords=[]
for r in json.loads((base/'agent3_magnus_as_nielsen.json').read_text())['words']:
 w=mul(*(NW[j] for j in r['endo_word']));z=evalfp(w);zz=gap.Image(gap.eval('ii4;'),z)
 ww=gap.LetterRepAssocWord(gap.UnderlyingElement(zz)).sage();IAwords.append(ww)
 print('IA mapped',r['generator_index'],'length',len(ww),time.time()-t,flush=True)
D['IAwords']=IAwords;(base/'agent4_K2_fp_data.json').write_text(json.dumps(D,default=int))
LP=gap.LPresentedGroup(gap.FreeGroupOfFpGroup(Kfp),gap.RelatorsOfFpGroup(Kfp),[],[])
for c in range(1,4):
 t0=time.time();ep=gap.NqEpimorphismNilpotentQuotient(LP,c);Q=gap.Range(ep)
 print('K2class',c,'seconds',time.time()-t0,'lcsinv',gap.AbelianInvariants(gap.LowerCentralSeries(Q)[c-1]),flush=True)
''')
import sys;sys.path.insert(0,'workspace')
exec(compile(p.read_text(),str(p),'exec'),{'__name__':'__main__','__file__':str(p.resolve())})
```

```
FILE gap/lib/grpnice.gi
InstallMethod(IsomorphismFpGroupByGeneratorsNC,"via niceomorphism/w. gens",
  IsFamFamX,[IsGroup and IsHandledByNiceMonomorphism, IsList,IsString],0,
function(g,c,nam)
local mon,iso;
  mon:=RestrictedNiceMonomorphism(g);
  c:=List(c,i->Image(mon,i));
  iso:=IsomorphismFpGroupByGeneratorsNC(NiceObject(g),c,nam);
  if iso=fail then
    return fail;
  else
    iso:=mon*iso;
    SetIsInjective(iso,true);
    SetIsSurjective(iso,true);
    ProcessEpimorphismToNewFpGroup(iso);
    mon:=MappingGeneratorsImages(iso);
    SetName(iso,Concatenation("<composed isomorphism:",
      String(mon[1]),"->",String(mon[2]),">"));
    return iso;
  fi;
end);

##

function( G )
local   nice,  img,  sub,i;
  nice := NiceMonomorphism(G);
  img  := ShallowCopy(CalcMaximalSubgroupClassReps( NiceObject(G) ));
  for i in [1..Length(img)] do
    sub  := GroupByNiceMonomorphism( nice, img[i] );
    SetParent( sub, G );
    img[i]:=sub;
  od;
  return img;
end );

    rels := RelatorsOfFpGroup( F );
    nr   := Length( rels );

    # build the relation matrix for the commutator  quotient  group
    M := [];
    for i in [ 1..Maximum( nr, ng ) ] do
      M[i] := List( [ 1..ng ], i->0 );
      if i <= nr then
        r := rels[i];
        for j in [1..NrSyllables(r)] do
          g := GeneratorSyllable(r,j);

      fi;

    # compute normal form
    norm := NormalFormIntMat( M,15 );
    D := norm.normal;
    Q := norm.coltrans;
    I := Q^-1;

    diag := List( [1..min], x -> D[x][x] );
    if ForAny( diag, x -> x = 0 ) then
        Info(InfoSQ,1,"solvable quotient is infinite");
```

```
BindGlobal( "SemidirectFp", function( G, aut, N )
local Go,No,giso,niso,FG,GP,FN,NP,F,GI,NI,rels,i,j,P;
  Go:=G;
  No:=N;
  if not IsFpGroup(G) then
    giso:=IsomorphismFpGroup(G);
  else
    giso:=IdentityMapping(G);
  fi;
  if not IsFpGroup(N) then
    niso:=IsomorphismFpGroup(N);
  else
    niso:=IdentityMapping(N);
  fi;
  G:=Image(giso,G);
  N:=Image(niso,N);

  FG:=FreeGeneratorsOfFpGroup(G);
  GP:=List(GeneratorsOfGroup(G),x->PreImagesRepresentative(giso,x));
  FN:=FreeGeneratorsOfFpGroup(N);
  NP:=List(GeneratorsOfGroup(N),x->PreImagesRepresentative(niso,x));

  F:=FreeGroup(List(Concatenation(FG,FN),String));
  GI:=GeneratorsOfGroup(F){[1..Length(FG)]};
  NI:=GeneratorsOfGroup(F){[Length(FG)+1..Length(GeneratorsOfGroup(F))]};

  rels:=[];
  for i in RelatorsOfFpGroup(G) do
    Add(rels,MappedWord(i,FG,GI));
  od;
  for i in RelatorsOfFpGroup(N) do
    Add(rels,MappedWord(i,FN,NI));
  od;
  for i in [1..Length(FG)] do
    for j in [1..Length(FN)] do
      Add(rels,NI[j]^GI[i]/(
        MappedWord(UnderlyingElement(Image(niso,Image(Image(aut,GP[i]),NP[j]))),FN,NI)  ));
    od;
  od;
  P:=F/rels;
  GI:=GeneratorsOfGroup(P){[1..Length(FG)]};
  NI:=GeneratorsOfGroup(P){[Length(FG)+1..Length(GeneratorsOfGroup(P))]};
  # set the embeddings and projections
  i:=rec(groups:=[Go,No],
         embeddings:=[GroupHomomorphismByImagesNC(Go,P,GP,GI),
                      GroupHomomorphismByImagesNC(No,P,NP,NI)],
         projections:=GroupHomomorphismByImagesNC(P,Go,
                        Concatenation(GI,NI),
                        Concatenation(GP,List(NI,x->One(Go))))  );
  SetSemidirectProductInfo(P,i);
  return P;
end );

#############################################################################
##
#F  PerfectGroup([<filter>,]<sz>,<nr>) . . . .  Access perfect groups library
##
InstallGlobalFunction( PerfectGroup, function(arg)
local func,sz,p;
  PerfGrpLoad(0); # force loading
  if not IsFunction(arg[1]) then
    func:=IsPermGroup;
    sz:=arg;
  else
    func:=arg[1];
    sz:=arg{[2..Length(arg)]};
  fi;
  if func=IsFpGroup then func:=IsSubgroupFpGroup;fi;
  # list given
  if Length(sz)=1 then
    if not IsList(sz[1]) then
      sz:=[sz[1],1];
    else
      sz:=sz[1];
    fi;
  fi;
  if sz[1] in PERFRec.notKnown then
    Error("Perfect groups of size ",sz[1]," not known");
  fi;
  p:=PerfGrpLoad(sz[1]);
  if p=fail or sz[2]>PERFRec.number[p] then
    Error("PerfectGroup(",sz[1],",",sz[2],") does not exist !");
  fi;
  p:=PerfGrpConst(func,PERFGRP[p][sz[2]]);
  SetSize(p,sz[1]);
  SetPerfectIdentification(p,ShallowCopy(sz));
  SetIsPerfectGroup(p,true);
  SetFilterObj(p,IsPerfectLibraryGroup);
  return p;
end );

##
#M  IsomorphismFpGroup   for perfect library groups
##
InstallMethod(IsomorphismFpGroup,"perfect library groups",true,
  [IsPerfectLibraryGroup],100,
function(G)
local H,hom,permgens,fpgens;
  H:=PerfectGroup(IsSubgroupFpGroup,PerfectIdentification(G));
  permgens:=GeneratorsOfGroup(G);
  fpgens:=GeneratorsOfGroup(H);
  if Length(permgens)<>Length(fpgens) then
    # remove auxiliary gens
    hom:=GroupHomomorphismByImagesNC(G,H,permgens{[1..Length(fpgens)]},fpgens);
  else
    hom:=GroupHomomorphismByImagesNC(G,H,permgens,fpgens);
  fi;
  SetIsInjective(hom,true);
  SetIsSurjective(hom,true);
  return hom;
end);

#############################################################################
##
#M  IsomorphismPermGroup   for perfect library groups
##
InstallMethod(IsomorphismPermGroup,"perfect library groups",true,
  [IsPerfectLibraryGroup],100,
function(G)
local H,hom,permgens,fpgens;
  H:=PerfectGroup(IsPermGroup,PerfectIdentification(G));
  fpgens:=GeneratorsOfGroup(G);
  permgens:=GeneratorsOfGroup(H);
  if Length(permgens)<>Length(fpgens) then
    # remove auxiliary gens
    hom:=GroupHomomorphismByImagesNC(G,H,fpgens,permgens{[1..Length(fpgens)]});
  else
    hom:=GroupHomomorphismByImagesNC(G,H,fpgens,permgens);
  fi;
  SetIsInjective(hom,true);
  SetIsSurjective(hom,true);
  return hom;
end);

  H:=PerfectGroup(IsSubgroupFpGroup,PerfectIdentification(G));
  permgens:=GeneratorsOfGroup(G);
  fpgens:=GeneratorsOfGroup(H);
  if Length(permgens)<>Length(fpgens) then
    # remove auxiliary gens

function(G)
local H,hom,permgens,fpgens;

    hom:=GroupHomomorphismByImagesNC(G,H,fpgens,permgens);
  fi;
  SetIsInjective(hom,true);
  SetIsSurjective(hom,true);
  return hom;
end);
```

```
##
##  methods to construct homomorphisms to fp groups
##
InstallOtherMethod(IsomorphismFpGroup,"subgroups of fp group",true,
  [IsSubgroupFpGroup,IsString],0,
function(u,str)
local aug,w,pres,f,fam,opt;
  if HasIsWholeFamily(u) and IsWholeFamily(u) then
    return IdentityMapping(u);
  fi;

  # catch trivial case of rank 0 group
  if Length(GeneratorsOfGroup(FamilyObj(u)!.wholeGroup))=0 then
    return IsomorphismFpGroup(FamilyObj(u)!.wholeGroup,str);
  elif Length( GeneratorsOfGroup( u ) ) = 0 or
       ( HasIsTrivial( u ) and IsTrivial( u ) ) then
    return GroupHomomorphismByImages( u, FreeGroup( 0 ), [], [] );
  fi;

  # get an augmented coset table from the group. Since we don't care about
  # any particular generating set, we let the function chose.
  aug:=AugmentedCosetTableInWholeGroup(u);

  Info( InfoFpGroup, 1, "Presentation with ",
    Length(aug.subgroupGenerators), " generators");

  # create a tietze object to reduce the presentation a bit
  if not IsBound(aug.subgroupRelators) then
    aug.subgroupRelators := RewriteSubgroupRelators( aug, aug.groupRelators);
  fi;

  # as the presentation might be rather long, we do not decode all secondary
  # generators and their images, but will do it ``on the fly'' when
  # rewriting.
  aug:=CopiedAugmentedCosetTable(aug);
  pres := PresentationAugmentedCosetTable( aug, "y",0# printlevel
                    ,true) ;# initialize tracking before the `1or2' routine!
  opt:=TzOptions(pres);

  if ValueOption("expandLimit")<>fail then
    opt.expandLimit:=ValueOption("expandLimit");
  else
    opt.expandLimit:=108; # do not grow too much.
  fi;
  if ValueOption("eliminationsLimit")<>fail then
    opt.eliminationsLimit:=ValueOption("eliminationsLimit");
  else
    opt.eliminationsLimit:=20; # do not be too greedy
  fi;
  if ValueOption("lengthLimit")<>fail then
    opt.lengthLimit:=ValueOption("lengthLimit");
  else
    opt.lengthLimit:=Int(3/2*pres!.tietze[TZ_TOTAL]); # not too big.
  fi;
  if ValueOption("generatorsLimit")<>fail then
    opt.generatorsLimit:=ValueOption("generatorsLimit");
  fi;

  TzOptions(pres).printLevel:=InfoLevel(InfoFpGroup);
  if ValueOption("cheap")=true then
    TzGo(pres);
  else
    TzEliminateRareOcurrences(pres,50);
    TzGoGo(pres); # cleanup
  fi;

  # new free group
  f:=FpGroupPresentation(pres,str);

  # images for the old primary generators
  aug.primaryImages:=Immutable(List(
        TzImagesOldGens(pres){[1..Length(aug.primaryGeneratorWords)]},
        i->MappedWord(i,GeneratorsOfPresentation(pres),GeneratorsOfGroup(f))));
  TrySecondaryImages(aug);
  # generator numbers of the new generators
  w:=List(TzPreImagesNewGens(pres),
          i->aug.treeNumbers[Position(OldGeneratorsOfPresentation(pres),i)]);

  # and the corresponding words in the original group
  w:=List(w,i->TreeRepresentedWord(aug.primaryGeneratorWords,aug.tree,i));
  if not IsWord(One(u)) then
    fam:=ElementsFamily(FamilyObj(u));
    w:=List(w,i->ElementOfFpGroup(fam,i));
  fi;

  # write the homomorphism in terms of the image's free generators
  # (so preimages are cheap)
  # this object cannot test whether it is a proper mapping, so skip
  # safety assertions that could be triggered by the construction process
  f:=GroupHomomorphismByImagesNC(u,f,w,GeneratorsOfGroup(f):noassert);
  # but give it `aug' as coset table, so we will use rewriting for images
  SetCosetTableFpHom(f,aug);

  SetIsBijective(f,true);

  return f;
end);

InstallOtherMethod(IsomorphismFpGroupByGeneratorsNC,"subgroups of fp group",
  IsFamFamX,
  [IsSubgroupFpGroup,IsList and IsMultiplicativeElementWithInverseCollection,
   IsObject],0,
function(u,gens,nam)
local aug,w,pres,f,trace;

  trace:=[];

  if HasIsWholeFamily(u) and IsWholeFamily(u) and
    IsIdenticalObj(gens,GeneratorsOfGroup(u)) then
      return IdentityMapping(u);
  fi;
  # get an augmented coset table from the group. It must be compatible with
  # `gens', so we must always use MTC.

  # use new MTC
  w:=FamilyObj(u)!.wholeGroup;
  aug:=NEWTC_CosetEnumerator(FreeGeneratorsOfFpGroup(w),
        RelatorsOfFpGroup(w),
        List(gens,UnderlyingElement),
        true,trace);

  pres:=NEWTC_PresentationMTC(aug,1,nam);
  if Length(GeneratorsOfPresentation(pres))>Length(gens) then
    aug:=NEWTC_CosetEnumerator(FreeGeneratorsOfFpGroup(w),
          RelatorsOfFpGroup(w),
          List(gens,UnderlyingElement),
          true,trace);

    pres:=NEWTC_PresentationMTC(aug,0,nam);
  fi;
  # check that we have the exact generators as we want and no rearrangement
  # or so happened.
  Assert(0,Length(GeneratorsOfPresentation(pres))=Length(gens)
    and pres!.primarywords=[1..Length(gens)]);

  # new free group
  f:=FpGroupPresentation(pres);
  aug.homgens:=gens;
  aug.homgenims:=GeneratorsOfGroup(f);
  aug.primaryImages:=GeneratorsOfGroup(f);
  aug.secondaryImages:=ShallowCopy(GeneratorsOfGroup(f));

  f:=GroupHomomorphismByImagesNC(u,f,gens,GeneratorsOfGroup(f):noassert);

  # tell f, that `aug' can be used as its coset table
  SetCosetTableFpHom(f,aug);

  SetIsBijective(f,true);

  return f;
end);

  fi;

  TzInitGeneratorImages(pres);
  if ValueOption("easy")=true then
```

```
import json
import sympy as S
from itertools import combinations

def inv(w):return tuple(-x for x in w[::-1])
def cat(*ws):
 out=[]
 for w in ws:
  for x in w:
   if out and out[-1]==-x:out.pop()
   else:out.append(x)
 return tuple(out)
def cm(a,b):return cat(a,b,inv(a),inv(b))
def fox(word,ts):
 # Fox derivative evaluated at commutative character xi->ts[i].
 ans=[0]*len(ts);p=1
 for x in word:
  i=abs(x)-1
  if x>0:ans[i]+=p;p*=ts[i]
  else:p//=ts[i];ans[i]-=p
 return ans
X=[(1,),(2,),(3,)];gens=[];names=[]
for i in range(3):
 for j in range(3):
  if i!=j:
   A=X.copy();A[i]=cat(X[j],X[i],inv(X[j]));gens.append(A);names.append(('C',i+1,j+1))
for i in range(3):
 j,k=[a for a in range(3) if a!=i];A=X.copy();A[i]=cat(cm(X[j],X[k]),X[i]);gens.append(A);names.append(('M',i+1,j+1,k+1))
ts=[1,-1,1];mat=[]
for name,A in zip(names,gens):
 J=S.Matrix([fox(w,ts) for w in A])
 assert J*S.Matrix([0,-2,0])==S.Matrix([0,-2,0])
 assert J[0,1]==J[2,1]==0
 block=J.extract([0,2],[0,2]).T
 mat.append(block);print(name,block.tolist())
D=S.diag(-1,1);N=-S.eye(2);U=mat[6];V=mat[8]
expected=[D,S.eye(2),S.eye(2),S.eye(2),S.eye(2),N*D,U,S.eye(2),V]
assert mat==expected
assert D*U*D==U.inv() and D*V*D==V.inv()
assert U.det()==V.det()==1
# Quotient free-product representation UT4(F2).
def mul(A,B):return (A*B).applyfunc(lambda x:x%2)
def invert(A):return A.inv().applyfunc(lambda x:x%2)
I=S.eye(4);a=I.copy();a[0,1]=1;b=I.copy();b[1,2]=1;c=I.copy();c[2,3]=1
d=a;u=mul(b,a);v=mul(c,a)
assert mul(d,d)==I and mul(mul(mul(d,u),d),u)==I and mul(mul(mul(d,v),d),v)==I
images=[d,I,I,I,I,d,u,I,v]
candidate=json.load(open('workspace/agent3_cubic_candidates.json'))['candidates'][0]['word']
R=I
for x in candidate:R=mul(R,images[x-1] if x>0 else invert(images[-x-1]))
assert R!=I
print('candidate image UT4(F2)',R.tolist())
assert R==I+S.eye(4)[:,0]*S.eye(4)[3,:]

from agent1_integral_derivations import bracket
G=[]
for name in names:
 if name[0]=='C':_,i,j=name;G.append({(i-1,(j-1,i-1)):1,(i-1,(i-1,j-1)):-1})
 else:_,i,j,k=name;G.append({(i-1,(j-1,k-1)):1,(i-1,(k-1,j-1)):-1})
J1=bracket(bracket(G[8],G[0]),G[6]);J2=bracket(bracket(G[6],G[0]),G[8])
```

```
def vector(p):
    v={}

        if len(u)==2:v[u[0]*N+u[1]]=c

def action(ws):
    ps=[magnus3(tuple(w[::-1]),N) for w in ws]
    lin=[{u:c for u,c in p.items() if len(u)==1} for p in ps]
    quad=[{u:c for u,c in p.items() if len(u)==2} for p in ps]
    act=[]
    for i,j in product(range(N),repeat=2):
        p={}
        for P,Q in [(lin[i],lin[j]),(quad[i],lin[j]),(lin[i],quad[j])]:
            for x,c in P.items():
                for y,d in Q.items():p[x+y]=p.get(x+y,0)+c*d

        p={}
        for x,c in lin[i].items():
            for y,d in lin[j].items():
                for z,e in lin[k].items():p[x+y+z]=p.get(x+y+z,0)+c*d*e
        act.append(vector(p))
    return act

def comm(v,k):
    ans={}

        if i>=off:continue
        a,b=divmod(i,N);j=off+(a*N+b)*N+k;l=off+(k*N+a)*N+b
        ans[j]=ans.get(j,0)+c;ans[l]=ans.get(l,0)-c
    return ans

for r in data['rels']:

while idx<len(L.queue):
```

```
from pathlib import Path
```
```
p=Path('workspace/agent4_arithmetic_audit.py')
p.write_text(r'''"""Exact finite checks for the arithmetic all-degree detector.
Classical global/local Hilbert and Matsumoto theorems are NOT simulated.
Their hypotheses are checked here; the general proof is in notes.
"""
from sympy import Matrix,eye,symbols,simplify,factor,factorint,Poly

def comm(A,B):return A*B*A.inv()*B.inv()
def Lbr(A,B):return A*B-B*A

a,b,u,x,pi,t=symbols('a b u x pi t')
X=eye(3);X[0,1]=a
Y=eye(3);Y[1,0]=b
Z=comm(X,Y)
v=1+a*b+a*a*b*b
assert Z==Matrix([[v,-a*a*b,0],[a*b*b,1-a*b,0],[0,0,1]])
LL=eye(3);LL[1,0]=a*b*b/v
DD=Matrix.diag(v,1/v,1)
UU=eye(3);UU[0,1]=-a*a*b/v
assert (LL*DD*UU-Z).applyfunc(simplify)==Matrix.zeros(3)
C=Matrix.diag(u,1,1/u)
assert comm(C,X)==Matrix([[1,(u-1)*a,0],[0,1,0],[0,0,1]])
# Coroot scalar product, crucial to avoid factor two.

# Johnson image computation in the pure-x2 current algebra.

 # Exact valuations in the Gauss decomposition of Z.

 # Choice of global field, all conductor and boundary hypotheses.
 odd=[p for p in factorint(c) if p%2]
 if odd:

 e=e0*r

  assert Poly(F,x).nth(0)==pi and Poly(F,x).nth(1)==pi

 # At r=1 take basefield itself and pi=zeta_p-1 or i-1.
```
